  ~ 'GROWTH ACT'!B3 [#DDEBF7]: '~UC_Sets: R_E: FI'
      -> =IF(Region_selection!$B$8=Region_selection!$D$8,"~UC_Sets: R_E: FI","~UC_Sets: R_S: AllRegions")
      (national constraint (sum over FI and the regions): same meaning as the single-region model)
  ~ 'GROWTH ACT'!N6 [#DDEBF7]: 'UC_RHSRT'
      -> =IF(Region_selection!$B$8=Region_selection!$D$8,"UC_RHSRT","UC_RHST")
      (national constraint: RHS without region)
  ~ 'GROWTH ACT'!O6 [#DDEBF7]: 'UC_RHSRT~0'
      -> =IF(Region_selection!$B$8=Region_selection!$D$8,"UC_RHSRT~0","UC_RHST~0")
      (national constraint: RHS without region)
